Re: City On Mars?




                                                         UFO Updates
                                   A mailing list for the study of UFO-related phenomena
                                               'Its All Here In Black & White'
    Location: UFOUpDatesList.Com > 2010 > May > May 7

                                                             Re: City On Mars?
  From: William Treurniet <wtreurniet.nul>
  Date: Thu, 06 May 2010 22:31:15 -0400
  Archived: Fri, 07 May 2010 08:21:51 -0400
  Subject: Re: City On Mars?

  >From: Joe Faccenda <Uforth.nul>
  >To: post.nul
  >Date: Wed, 5 May 2010 19:43:21 EDT
  >Subject: City On Mars?
  >As was pointed out to me by a keen-eyed retired Archeologist
  >what appears to be a large grid of buildings/City on a photo
  >from the Mars Express Spacecraft from the Hale Crater.
  >It could be no more than natural terrain features.
  >However I do think it is of interest.
  >I have a compilation at:
  >http://www.uforth.com/bird%20skull.htm
  >Scroll down to view
  >Or view the full Mars photo on the ESA site at:
  >http://tiny.cc/ediro
  >The image was on NASA's site but has been taken off.
  I and a colleague became immersed in studying the ESA Mars
  Express Hale Crater image in 2008. We too were fascinated by the
  rectangular line patterns on the crater floor. We eventually
  concluded that the patterns were artifacts due to the image
  processing model used to reconstruct the terrain from the raw
  data.
  My colleague queried a member of the German HRSC camera team
  requesting an explanation. The reply was as follows:
  "concerning         your question about the features in the images:
  The features/block artifacts are basically the result of two
  points.
  1. The image data is compressed onboard Mars Express using a DCT
  (JPEG) algorithm.
  2. Not optimal determined Digital Terrain Models (DTMs) in plane
  areas leads to block artifacts too."
  A discussion of the Digital Terrain Model used to process the
  data from the Mars Express High Resolution Stereo Camera (HRSC)
  may be found at the following link.
  http://spacespin.org/article.php/80209-new-dtm-mars
  In a FAQ at:
  http://hrscview.fu-berlin.de/faq.html
  (no longer available?) we are told that relief features which
  don't correspond to the image can occur. "The elevation model is
  derived from stereo matching, a technique which works very well


http://www.ufoupdateslist.com/2010/may/m07-008.shtml[10/12/2011 21:06:00]
Re: City On Mars?

  in regions with many contrasting edges. In smooth, featureless
  areas, or when the surface contrast is reduced by atmospheric
  dust, it becomes harder to match the stereo channels (the human
  eye has the same difficulty), so these areas have to be
  interpolated. Spurious matches in low-texture areas can cause
  artefacts in the generated elevation model."
  What this says is that incorrect relief features can appear in
  the calculated image where the surface is smooth, i.e.,
  practically featureless. This might be the case in the bottom of
  the crater where we see the geometric patterns. On a relatively
  featureless surface, there can be differences between the stereo
  channels that measure elevation, so the interpolated elevation
  might bounce up and down between successive measurements. If a
  lighting model is applied when creating the perspective view, a
  small change in elevation might cause a change in brightness
  depending on the new estimated slope and the direction of the
  light source. If the interpolated elevation is the same in
  successive horizontal scans due to quantization, thicker shapes
  of constant brightness such as rectangles might appear in the
  image.
  In my own analysis of the image, I increased the contrast in the
  red, green, and blue channels independently using equalization.
  The patterns were clearly evident in the green and blue
  channels, but not at all in the red channel. Further, the
  patterns in the green channel were very different from the
  patterns in the blue channel. This is consistent with the
  explanation given to us that the DTM was responsible for the
  unnatural features. If different data streams were used to
  generate each channel, or if the DTM used slightly different
  algorithms for each channel, then the model would yield
  different quantization levels in each channel. This argues that
  the patterns are not related to anything real on the surface of
  the crater. Otherwise, the channels would show similar patterns.
  These kinds of anomalies can be found in Google Earth images as
  well. The following links show pictures of like patterns in
  lakes in the Andes.
  Unidentified Underwater Anomalies
  http://creatorsdream.com/v/UWA/
  Atlantis, UFO bases or Ancient Civilizations Underwater in the
  Andes of Peru?
  http://www.creatorsdream.com/node/18
  Underwater Anomalies in the Andes of Peru YouTube Video
  http://www.creatorsdream.com/node/20
  The presence of these artifacts on the surface of water would
  suggest that Google Earth images are also generated using a DTM.
  Like the bottom of the Hale Crater, the surface of the water
  would be smooth, and could again result in the observed
  quantization effects.

  William Treurniet

Re: The Nature Of Ufology And Ufologists




                                                         UFO Updates
                                   A mailing list for the study of UFO-related phenomena

                                           Re: The Nature Of Ufology And Ufologists
  From: Martin Shough <parcellular.nul>
  Date: Fri, 7 May 2010 11:43:02 +0100
  Archived: Fri, 07 May 2010 08:24:46 -0400
  Subject: Re: The Nature Of Ufology And Ufologists

  >From: Joe McGonagle <joe.mcgonagle.nul>
  >To: post.nul
  >Date: Thu, 06 May 2010 03:42:43 +0100
  >Subject: Re: The Nature Of Ufology And Ufologists
  >I'll try one more time. Let's compare the 'UFO-subculture' with
  >the 'fairy subculture' based on the literature.
  >In the fairy literature, how many of the following appear?
  I like quizzes. I thought it would be fun to see how far I could
  get:
  >- animal mutilations
  tick that one, yes. Fairies would take animals and leave their
  eyeless remains in the fields
  >- encounters with fairies uncovered by HR
  hypnotism is relatively recent in terms of the history of fairy
  mythology and so the motif did not have the opportunity to
  become embedded in its development. There may be some modern
  esotericists who have used it. Does anyone know?
  >- abductions (that is one)
  yes it is
  >- claims that the fairies built the Pyramids or Stonehenge
  I think you could find such connections, actually, in some of
  the esoteric literature.
  >- a global cover-up about the existence of fairies
  The idea of global govt or global anything did not really exist
  until recent times, so a global political/military cover-up
  could not have been associated with fairy beliefs. But there is
  a long tradition of esoteric secret knowledge and secret
  societies which has interseced with the fairy beliefs, and
  communication with spirit entities has in general been
  associated with secret priveleges of a priest or shaman cast in
  all cultures.
  >- a human-usable technology for free energy
  can't think of anything for that one, but...
  >- fairies forcibly impregnate women then steal the foetuses
  This is not so far off the mark. The fairies did steal human
  women for the specific purpose of making them have fairy
  children, which might then be 'raken' in their turn
  >- faked official documents
  again, not a hit - but public officials were (AFAIK) never
  obliged to document fairy lore in any organised way - excepting


http://www.ufoupdateslist.com/2010/may/m07-009.shtml[10/12/2011 21:06:00]
Re: The Nature Of Ufology And Ufologists

  perhaps the records of the witchcraft trials, which contain many
  fairy accounts - and the idea of 'official documents' would
  hardly have been on the radar during most of fairy history. This
  is really an addendum to the "global cover-up" issue.
  >- accusations that the British Royal family are fairies
  funny you should mention it, but I've always had this suspicion
  about... no, never mind ;-)
  >- a catalogue of 57 varieties of fairy
  if you imply that fairy morphology lacks variety compared to
  ufonauts' descriptions, this is hard to quantify but I think you
  might well be wrong. There is a huge number of different types
  and forms around the world, and even within each small
  subculture (say, Celtic lore) there is plenty of variety. I
  won't start a list though
  >- a fairy crash/retrieval
  crashed fairies? A bizzare notion. But are there stories of found
  fairy artefacts etc
  >- fairies (okay that's another match)
  ?
  >- people have been killed because they knew too much about
  >fairies
  something like this is present in the Celtic beliefs, and
  probably elsewhere, where seers may be harmed - badly injured or
  terminally 'taken' - for interfering in fairy business or
  spreading fairy secrets
  >- fairies are engaged in global monitoring of the population
  again I don't think the notion of "global monitoring" belongs in
  the same thought-universe as the historical fairy faith(s), but
  it wsa certainly the case that communities would believe that
  the fairies knew what they were doing and saying, and they would
  be spoken of even privately in very cautious terms, rather as
  though the walls were bugged
  >- telepathic contact with fairies
  accounts of mental communication with fairies are quite common
  >- remote viewers touring fairy cities
  again "remote viewers" did not exist until very recently, and
  fairy dwelling s were never typically cities. But the old idea
  of clairvoyant vision is clearly associated with fairy beliefs,
  and many visits to fairy dwellings do occur in dream or vision.
  >- fairies shadowed the moon-missions
  it wouldn't surprise me if there are such claims out there, but
  I think the underlying point - that ufonauts _did_ allegedly
  shadow the moon missions - more pertinently illustrates the
  cultural continuum embracing both fairies and ufonauts, and the
  latter could be said to be standing in for the former in such a
  case
  >- there is a global media blackout on the topic of fairies
  there was no global media during 99% of the historical
  development of fairy mythology. Again this is a subvariant of
  the the above global-monitoring/global cover-up points
  >- fairies are concerned about nuclear warfare
  fairies were analogously regarded as expressing a direct concern
  about damaging human behaviour, but of course the scale was
  local and the focus more moral and less technological. Humans
  would be visited and warned to mend their ways - to shape up
  personally, or not to damage the environment (also the fairies'
  environment, containing their paths, trees, hills etc).
  >- fairies shot down 1,000 US war planes


http://www.ufoupdateslist.com/2010/may/m07-009.shtml[10/12/2011 21:06:00]
Re: The Nature Of Ufology And Ufologists


  mmm
  >- fairies created a global network of energy lines
  Yes, the idea of ley lines as energy paths or as relics of some
  magical technology been rather widely connected with the fairy
  traditions
  >- fairies are all-powerful benevolent beings (another one)
  no fairy varieties were all-powerful - they were much more
  qualified creatures, quasi-human in many ways although
  supernatural .They were not gods. But in some esoteric
  traditions they have been associated in a chain of being with
  godlike entities, and might enact the wills of gods - in general
  angels stand in the fairy tradition. I think a similar picture
  could be drawn from some contactee literature
  >- fairies are malevolent beings
  Yes, they often are. They are various in type and nature,
  complex in character and saocial structure, and have all the
  different characteristics of humans
  >- the SDI was set up to defend us against fairies
  that's certainly one I've never come across! I can't think of an
  analogous claim in the fairy lore, either
  >- fairies are our progenitors
  There are many different theories of fairy origins in different
  places. They were most widely believed to be a race apart, but
  ancient, and intimately connected with human origins through
  religious theories, as in the idea that they were the fallen
  angels cast out of heaven, or that the fairies were the souls of
  the dead. It was generally thought that was an ancient bond and
  that in some way men and faeries were symbiotic, and needed one
  another, as expressed in the idea that the fairies needed human
  mothers who would bear their children. The technical idea of
  genetic continuity obviously could not have occurred to the
  percipients and story-tellers who wove the myths.
  >- fairies can defy physical laws (another one)
  Yes.
  >- crop circles are made by fairies (possibly another one)
  Most emphatically. They were the original Dougs and Daves!
  >- military jets have been sent to intercept fairies
  I assume this arises from some idea that traditional fairies are
  flying creatures with wings? This arose by a late literary
  conflation with the Victorian Chritsmas-tree angel but was not a
  spontaneous feature of traditionl "reports". They did sometimes
  travel in whirlwinds and the like and could occasionally be seen
  as flying lights etc, but generally speaking military jets have
  been rare motifs in fairy lore!
  >- Jesus Christ was a fairy
  Fairy lore is a broad church and the cross-overs with
  Christianised esoteric traditions contain all kinds of
  connections. I wouldn't rule out being able to find a thread
  leading to this idea.
  >- transistors are fairy technology
  again, fairy artefacts have been recovered and fairy gifts
  aplenty received. Not transistors, but almost-equally useful
  things like self-replenishing food bowls. Most fairy technology
  turns out to be useless when you metaphorically plug it in - it
  famously turns to crap or dust. Much the same can be said of
  ufological artefacts. In a less technological age the benefits
  of retro-engineered fairy products tended to be more software
  than hardware - typically tunes, songs, riddles and poems. Many
  fine tunes were said to have been overheard from or gifted by
  fairies.


http://www.ufoupdateslist.com/2010/may/m07-009.shtml[10/12/2011 21:06:00]
Re: The Nature Of Ufology And Ufologists


  >- the fairy autopsy film
  >- faked fairy photographs (there's another one)
  Yep.
  >- a fairy was present on 9/11
  >- and so on, and so on, and so on. The list is not exhaustive.
  But is long enough to give credence to the idea that ufological
  beliefs probably are in an indirect line of descent from the
  fairy beliefs, or at least that both descend from a common
  ancestor. I merely observe this. I don't say anything about what
  it may or may not imply for your argument.

  Best regards
  Martin

  From: Stanton T. Friedman <fsphys.nul>
  Date: Fri, 7 May 2010 10:22:59 -0300

  Subject: Re: City On Mars?

  >From: Carol Maltby <carolmaltby.nul>

  >>I have a compilation at:
  >>http://www.uforth.com/bird%20skull.htm
  ><snip>
  >First of all, a recommendation for all presentations based on
  >satellite or ground-level Mars images: always include the image
  >number and URL where it can be found (or was found in the past,
  >in case it disappears, which they sometimes do).
  >If it's worth making a presentation on, it's worth knowing what
  >you are talking about so that you can answer any rebuttals you
  >may receive.
  >Number one thing you need to know before getting excited about
  >any Mars anomalies is how to distinguish digital artifacts from
  >what may be actual features. In this case, that the area that
  >looks like a city is made up of strips should be a red flag.
  >When you start enlarging it further, you'll see that it totally
  >breaks down into a geometric crazy quilt of digital patterns
  >that have nothing to do with depicting any natural or artificial
  >feature. A look at the YouTube video (warning: electronic music
  >abuse ahead!) "Mars City Inside the Hale Crater" will give you a
  >vivid example of what not to do.
  >http://www.youtube.com/watch?v=uS_K-KAFKJg
  >If a Mars "city" looks very ordered in one area, follow it out
  >to its "suburbs" and see what happens to it on the edges or
  >where it meets incompatible terrain. Sometimes an area that
  >looks pretty rectilinear in one section will naturally degrade
  >into totally chaotic, obviously natural terrain. Some people
  >will just present the most regularly geometric section cropped
  >out of its context. That's nothing short of dishonest.
  >In the case of the Hale Crater image, see how your little
  >rectilinear city features manage to continue up the side of a
  >hill as if the terrain going vertical was no barrier at all.
  >What's the scale of the "buildings"? It's your responsibility to
  >indicate what size your buildings are, so others can determine
  >how credible the claim is.
  >It's always mandatory to consider sun and camera angles to see
  >if they are congruent with your assumptions about what you are
  >seeing. If the camera is right overhead, anything that appears
  >to be a perspective showing a roof and side walls can be ruled


http://www.ufoupdateslist.com/2010/may/m07-010.shtml[10/12/2011 21:06:02]
Re: City On Mars?

  >out.
  >In your Hale Crater image, you've neglected to check the
  >directions of the shadows. If we call the source image to be
  >oriented with north at the top (for the sake of argument), the
  >sun is shining from the southeast with the shadows in the
  >northwest. Many of the rectilinear components that look like
  >buildings appear to have "shadows" pointing to the south, which
  >would mean a light source to the north. Real features don't have
  >random light sources. In some cases, checking shadows is what
  >you'll need to do to determine whether a feature is a dome or a
  >crater.
  >Being able to view Mars and examine sections of it closely is
  >one of the glories of being alive in this time and place. There
  >are some very unusual features worthy of more study, and I don't
  >rule out possibilities of some being intelligently constructed.
  >But if you are looking for signs of ruined constructed artifacts
  >you have to be able to observe accurately, to describe what you
  >observe accurately, and to be able to place it in a context that
  >rules out natural origin. There are routine objections that
  >always come up regarding anomalies. You've got to be able to
  >know them, and counter them. I don't think you've made a
  >persuasive case here on any level.
  >I'd love to have input from archeologists who have training and
  >experience in working with satellite images in an archeological
  >context, and who are willing to put their names and professional
  >credibility on the line when proposing that some Mars features
  >may be non-natural. But in the 12 years I've been following Mars
  >anomalies, to the best of my knowledge there has not been a
  >single example of an archeologist publicly discussing criteria
  >for assessing Mars anomalies. I expect that for one of them to
  >discuss what the criteria would be for determining whether the
  >Face on Mars is a large eroded sculptural form would be
  >professional suicide.
  What a pleasure to read Carol's oh so sensible comments about
  image evaluation. I get a lot of requests for opinions on
  digital photos about which almost no real information is
  provided. My gray basket keeps getting larger.
  Thanks much, Carol.

  Stan Friedman

  From: William Treurniet <wtreurniet.nul>

  >From: Chris Rutkowski <canadianuforeport.nul>

  >>Subject: City On Mars?
  >>I have a compilation at:
  >>http://www.uforth.com/bird%20skull.htm
  >You've got to be kidding.
  >I suppose ufologists and keen-eyed archeologists need courses in
  >image processing and the production of processing artefacts.
  The image was obtained with the Mars Express High Resolution
  Stereo Camera (HRSC). This camera records altitude data for
  every point the camera sees. The altitude data is then used to
  make a Digital Terrain Model to create perspective views of the
  surface. The artifacts in Hale Crater appear to be due to
  quantization effects resulting from processing adjacent altitude
  measurements of a smooth surface.
  http://spacespin.org/article.php/80209-new-dtm-mars
  I think its a bit much to expect all of us to be up on all areas
  of expertise such as the latest image processing techniques. The
  alternative is to do as Mr. Faccenda did and ask if others have
  an explanation. That seems like a reasonable use for this list.

  William Treurniet

  >looks pretty rectilinear in one section will naturally degrade
  >into totally chaotic, obviously natural terrain. Some people
  >will just present the most regularly geometric section cropped
  >out of its context. That's nothing short of dishonest.
  >In the case of the Hale Crater image, see how your little
  >rectilinear city features manage to continue up the side of a
  >hill as if the terrain going vertical was no barrier at all.
  Actually, the patterns don't go that much off the crater floor.
  The little they do is no argument against city features since


http://www.ufoupdateslist.com/2010/may/m08-002.shtml[10/12/2011 21:06:03]
Re: City On Mars?

  they would be part of an alien architectural style. It's not
  unheard of even on earth to find hillside or cliffside
  dwellings.
  >What's the scale of the "buildings"? It's your responsibility to
  >indicate what size your buildings are, so others can determine
  >how credible the claim is.
  The resolution of the elevation data in the Mars Express Digital
  Terrain Model is 50 m, with a height accuracy of 10 m. Heights
  of buildings would have to be huge to be determined from this
  data. The lengths and widths have a resolution of 10 m/pixel.
  http://spacespin.org/article.php/80209-new-dtm-mars
  >It's always mandatory to consider sun and camera angles to see
  >if they are congruent with your assumptions about what you are
  >seeing. If the camera is right overhead, anything that appears
  >to be a perspective showing a roof and side walls can be ruled
  >out.
  >In your Hale Crater image, you've neglected to check the
  >directions of the shadows. If we call the source image to be
  >oriented with north at the top (for the sake of argument), the
  >sun is shining from the southeast with the shadows in the
  >northwest. Many of the rectilinear components that look like
  >buildings appear to have "shadows" pointing to the south, which
  >would mean a light source to the north. Real features don't have
  >random light sources. In some cases, checking shadows is what
  >you'll need to do to determine whether a feature is a dome or a
  >crater.
  The patterns look 2D to me. Seeing shadows means that one has
  already decided that the patterns have a third dimension. So
  shadows cannot be used to "prove" that there are buildings
  there. But I agree that the inconsistencies you describe may be
  used to cast considerable doubt on it.
  >Being able to view Mars and examine sections of it closely is
  >one of the glories of being alive in this time and place. There
  >are some very unusual features worthy of more study, and I don't
  >rule out possibilities of some being intelligently constructed.
  >But if you are looking for signs of ruined constructed artifacts
  >you have to be able to observe accurately, to describe what you
  >observe accurately, and to be able to place it in a context that
  >rules out natural origin. There are routine objections that
  >always come up regarding anomalies. You've got to be able to
  >know them, and counter them. I don't think you've made a
  >persuasive case here on any level.
  I agree. The web page merely presented the picture and asked the
  question. I assume the author did not know how to answer it
  himself, hence, his query to the List.
  >I'd love to have input from archeologists who have training and
  >experience in working with satellite images in an archeological
  >context, and who are willing to put their names and professional
  >credibility on the line when proposing that some Mars features
  >may be non-natural. But in the 12 years I've been following Mars
  >anomalies, to the best of my knowledge there has not been a
  >single example of an archeologist publicly discussing criteria
  >for assessing Mars anomalies. I expect that for one of them to
  >discuss what the criteria would be for determining whether the
  >Face on Mars is a large eroded sculptural form would be
  >professional suicide.
  Besides being impossible. How could an archeologist trained on
  past earth cultures and artifacts have any idea what the
  artifacts of an alien biology and culture would look like?

  William Treurniet

  and can read a persons mind. Also please note that with implants
  the typical humans becomes a combination cell phone and
  computer. Thus the person becomes a walking talking revealer of
  secrets. This person is concerned that state secrets might have

  a question and the answer to their question will typically
  follow to them from your mind.

  Extraterrestrials but the soviet government who could send this
  person to a Gulag in Siberia for revealing a secret which is
  impossible to keep.
  The extraterrestrials have beaming technology, can walk through
  walls and be invisible your presence. With these points in mind
  a secret cannot be kept from them. So don't worry about keeping
  a secret, for that is impossible.

  Good stuff!

  Some of you may not be familiar with extraterrestrial
  talents. Please read thie following article and my
  comments at the end.
  -----
  The MP has written a letter to Mr Medvedev raising a list of his
  concerns.
  In his letter he says that - assuming the whole thing was not
  just a bad joke - it was an historic event and should have been
  reported to the Kremlin.
  He also asks if there are official guidelines for what
  government officials should do if contacted by aliens,
  especially if those officials have access to state secrets.
  Mr Ilyumzhinov said in an interview on primetime television that
  he had been taken on board an alien spaceship which had come to
  planet Earth to take samples - and claims to have several
  witnesses.
  He has been president of Kalmykia, a small Buddhist region of
  Russia which lies on the shores of the Caspian Sea, for 17
  years.
  The millionaire former businessman has a reputation as an
  eccentric character.
  As president of the World Chess Federation, he has spent tens of
  millions of dollars turning the impoverished republic into a
  mecca for chess players - building an entire village to host
  international tournaments.
  -----
  Please keep in mind that the extraterrestrials are telepathic
  and can read a person's mind. Also please note that with
  implants the typical human can become a combination cell phone
  and computer. Thus the person becomes a walking talking revealer
  of secrets. This person is concerned that state secrets might
  have been revealed. The assumption with extraterrestrials should
  be that they know everything that you know. On the plus side no
  one is ever tortured to acquire information. All the
  extraterrestrials have to do is read your mind after thinking of
  a question and the answer to their question will typically
  follow from your mind.
  This persons real concern should not be the Extraterrestrials,
  but the soviet government who could send this person to a Gulag
  in Siberia for revealing secrets which are impossible to keep.
  Also please note the extraterrestrials have beaming technology,
  can walk through walls and be invisible in your presence. With
  these points in mind a secret cannot be kept from them. So don't
  worry about keeping secrets, for that is not possible.

  Lyle Michel

  Subject: Alien Teeth?

  EBK, List,
  Barney Hill had dentures and Betty reported that the aliens
  examining him were much "taken aback" when they discovered that
  his teeth were removable.
  I have a serious question for the List...
  Do any of you know of photos or drawings of ET/ aliens which
  show their teeth, or indicate that they even have teeth? Do you
  know of any abductee or other testimony that describes alien
  teeth?
  The more commonly-reported small and tall gray aliens appear to
  have mouth-like structures with no tongues or teeth. I'm
  searching for exceptions.

  Thanks,
  Ed

                                   Alamo Nevada Conference Memorial Weekend 2010
  From: Lyle Michel <ljm1915.nul>
  Date: Sat, 8 May 2010 11:38:20 -0700
  Archived: Sun, 09 May 2010 06:04:12 -0400
  Subject: Alamo Nevada Conference Memorial Weekend 2010

  Alamo Conference Alamo Nevada
  Memorial Weekend 2010.
  I just received and e-mail from Ike Bishop who is hosting the
  Alamo conference in Alamo Nevada which is about 120 miles from
  Las Vegas and about 40 miles from Rachel Nevada and area 51. Ike
  indicated he has a couple of rooms left in case any one on the
  List is interested in attending. Ike may be contacted at his
  e-mail address which is alamoconference.nul
  The dates of the conference are May 28, 29th, and 30th, 2010.
  This year the speakers will be Barbara Lamb, Michelle Emerson,
  Travis Walton, Jim Moroney, and Cynthia Crawford.
  I have had the privilege of hearing Barbara Lamb speak on
  several occasions at the International UFO Congress in Laughlin
  Nevada (most recently 2010). This year she spoke about crop
  circles. Also at this conference I attended her hosting of a
  small gathering of experiencers (persons that have been with the
  extraterrestrials) over several evenings of the week-long
  conference. During these sessions experiencers had an
  opportunity to tell their stories about being taken and spending
  time with these beings from other worlds.
  As many of you know people who have been with our other worldly
  guests often have difficulty remembering their experiences.
  Barbara Lamb as a hypnotherapist has regressed a number of these
  people so that they can better recall their experiences.
  In her book Alien Experiences she has discussed 25 cases of
  close encounters never before revealed. This book is available
  for purchase at her web site:
  http://alienexperiences.com/
  At the 2009 International UFO Congress Barbara Lamb
  spent about an hour and a half talking about
  some of the experiences people have had
  while being with these beings
  from other planets. She also presented drawings of
  something like 90 + beings from
  other worlds. If I recall correctly 2 of these pictures
  were thought to be actual photos
  of extraterrestrials.
  I have been a student of the extraterrestrial phenomena for
  about 19 years and am convinced beyond a reasonable doubt that
  beings from other worlds are among us and frequently travel from
  their home worlds to earth to view and experience our
  civilization. These other worldly cultures are different from us
  and my feeling is that we can best begin the process of
  understanding these cultures through the people that have been
  with these other worldly beings.
  Therefore, I would like to encourage those of you who want to
  know more about our alien friends to come to Ike Bishop's Alamo
  conference and here Barbara Lamb speak. I feel she is an expert
  in her field and you will be enlightened by her presentations.


http://www.ufoupdateslist.com/2010/may/m09-002.shtml[10/12/2011 21:06:06]

  Additional detail about the other conference speakers is
  available at:
  http://alamo-ufoconference.webs.com/speakers.htm
  Hotel reservation information is also available at the above web
  site.
  Lyle Michel

http://www.ufoupdateslist.com/2010/may/m11-003.shtml[10/12/2011 21:06:08]
Re: City On Mars?

  >http://tinyurl.com/29nx473
  Nature doesn't drop single, intact gerbil legs on the surface of
  barren deserts. If Nature has gerbils running around, Nature
  includes microorganisms that will break down flesh to bone and
  ever smaller components. The relentless dusty winds of Mars that
  shape features large and small would long ago have ground down
  body parts.
  In order to be special, this rock would have to be different.
  Look at the rocks around it. The right angle may remind you of
  the leg structure of many mammals. But see how many rocks in
  just that small cropped section have a right angle as part of
  their structure. I see over a dozen right angles in that section
  alone, and that says to me it is a typical way the rock
  fractures there.
  While the lower leg and paw are the most realistic simulacra,
  the geometric, flattened plane of the "thigh" is completely
  wrong for a leg, especially with that prominent flat
  quadrilateral feature. Again, it shows the same characteristic
  shearing on a flat plane, perpendicular to the right angles, as
  many of the rocks around it. The small striations giving the
  impression of "toes" are common to most of the rocks there.
  This mania for deciding that if Mars features, from individual
  rocks to large landscapes, "look like" Earth animals they must
  be depictions of Earth animals, is one of the worst mistakes the
  Mars anomalies community has made over the years. It's
  particularly embarrassing because it not only shows poor
  observation, but unwillingness to do some elementary researching
  into animal anatomy to back it up.
  George Haas draws some lines around a large landscape feature
  that he says is a bas relief of a parrot, and has a veterinarian
  confirm that he's right (George has an ethical lapse by
  neglecting to say that the vet in question is actually his
  wife). But the structure of the feet as he has drawn it, and the
  vet signed off on it, is anatomically impossible if you are a
  member of the parrot family. He then changed his drawing to make
  it reflect the correction! John Levassuer of SPSR was pushing a
  large surface feature as being an accurate depiction of a
  "puma". In this case, it was a puma seemingly un-troubled by
  the fact that one of its legs was on backward. It's maddening.
  "Looks like" does not mean it "is".
  Bert, the actual structure of the other small rocky shape you
  link to is just too close to the limits of resolution to be of
  any use as an example. There is simply no way to even see what
  aspects may be individual rocks in the background that are
  giving the false appearance of being a part of a more intricate
  shape, or what seeming features are due to pixilation.

  Carol Maltby

                                          Statement On ETs By Rep. Henry McElroy
  From: Larry W Bryant <overtci.nul>
  Date: Wed, 12 May 2010 01:48:54 -0400
  Archived: Wed, 12 May 2010 07:49:37 -0400
  Subject: Statement On ETs By Rep. Henry McElroy

  Here's a forward of the e-mail sent to me early this morning.
  On Wed, May 12, 2010 at 12:32 AM, [the video-crew chief] wrote:
  -----
  This is a transcript of the video made on May 8, 2010, at Fort
  Monroe, Virginia by Henry W. McElroy, Jr., Retiring
  New Hampshire State Representative.
  The video can be viewed at:
  http://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3DmrK2YgfjnHo
  Hello, I'm speaking with you from Fort Monroe, Virginia. We're
  also known as The Gateway to Freedom.
  We sit right next to a little town called Phoebus, Virginia.
  Interestingly, they call themselves The Gateway To The New
  World.
  Phoebus sits inside of Hampton, Virginia whose motto is First
  =46rom The Sea, First To The Stars.
  My name is Henry McElroy, Jr. a retiring, former State
  Representative from New Hampshire. Thank you for your attention
  to this brief message concerning the world's interaction with
  both earth-based, and off-world astronauts.
  The reason I am making this announcement is in the hopes of
  encouraging better perspective to all who explore the universe.
  Therefore, as a result, making a more valuable contribution to
  humanity, for the benefit of all faiths, all races and all
  nations.
  Another reason I am here today is because I believe in the
  foundational wisdom of our nation, which was laid down by our
  founding fathers and because we know that highly advanced
  knowledge and information can assist human beings in solving
  various problems both in current times, and in our future.
  When I was in the New Hampshire State Legislature, I served on
  the State Federal Relations and Veterans Affairs committee. It
  was, apparently, important that as a Representative of the
  Sovereign People who had elected me to this Honorable Office,
  that I be updated on a large number of topics related to the
  affairs of our People, and our Nation. As I understood it, some
  of those ongoing topics had been examined and categorized as
  Federal, State, Local development, and security matters. These
  documents related to various topics some of which spanned
  decades of our nation's history. One of those recurring topics
  is the reason I am addressing you this evening.
  I would like to submit to our nation my personal testimony of
  one document related to one of these ongoing topics which I saw
  while in office, serving on the State Federal Relations and
  Veterans Affairs Committee.
  The document I saw was an official brief to President


http://www.ufoupdateslist.com/2010/may/m12-001.shtml[10/12/2011 21:06:09]
Statement On ETs By Rep. Henry McElroy

  Eisenhower. To the best of my memory this brief was pervaded
  with a sense of hope, and it informed President Eisenhower of
  the continued presence of extraterrestrial beings here in the
  United States of America.
  The brief seemed to indicate that a meeting between the
  President and some of these visitors could be arranged as
  appropriate if desired.
  The tone of the brief indicated to me that there was no need for
  concern, since these visitors were in no way, causing any harm,
  or had any intentions, whatsoever, of causing any disruption
  then, or in the future.
  While I can't verify the times or places or that any meeting or
  meetings occurred directly between Eisenhower and these visitors
  =96 because of his optimism in his farewell address in 1961, I
  personally believe that Eisenhower did, indeed, meet with these
  extraterrestrial, off world astronauts.
  I hope my personal testimony will aid the nation in its quest
  for continued enlightenment. I am honored to follow in the
  footsteps of those who have come forward with their personal
  testimonies =96 those who deserve the admiration of the American
  people for sharing their accounts publicly, in an effort to
  elevate our knowledge to a higher understanding of our
  existence.
  People such as:
  Former Astronauts
  John Glenn
  Edgar Mitchell
  Gordon Cooper
  and
  Buzz Aldrin
  Former Presidents
  Ronald Reagan
  and
  Jimmy Carter
  Captain Bill Uhouse of the United States Marine Corps
  Lt. Col John Williams of the United States Air Force
  Co Phillip Corso Sr. of the United States Army
  Commander Graham Bethune of the United States Navy
  Along with:
  David Hamilton of the Department of Energy
  Donna Hare of NASA
  and
  James Kopf of the National Security Agency
  I would also like to thank the countries of:
  France
  Brazil
  Britain
  Russia
  Italy
  Denmark

  Norway
  New Zealand

  Uruguay

  Australia

  countries, and allowing them access to information that is so
  very important to the evolution of humanity.
  I thank you for allowing me this opportunity to have a small
  part in doing the same, by sharing the information I have given
  you today.
  Thank you very much and I'd like to thank our communications

  And, I'd also like to authorize distribution of this video for

  Thank you.

NASA Spacecraft 'Hijacked By Aliens'




                                                         UFO Updates
                                   A mailing list for the study of UFO-related phenomena
                                               'Its All Here In Black & White'
    Location: UFOUpDatesList.Com > 2010 > May > May 13

                                             NASA Spacecraft 'Hijacked By Aliens'
  From: Joe McGonagle <joe.mcgonagle.nul>
  Date: Thu, 13 May 2010 17:43:33 +0100
  Archived: Thu, 13 May 2010 13:06:45 -0400
  Subject: NASA Spacecraft 'Hijacked By Aliens'

  Source: www.news.com.au
  http://tinyurl.com/3y6z857
  May 13, 2010
  [Note: the source cited in the article is 'Bild', a German
  version of the British 'Sun' comic]
  NASA Spacecraft 'Hijacked By Aliens'
  A German UFO expert has claimed that a NASA spacecraft may have
  been hijacked by aliens - who are now attempting to contact
  Earth.
  Hartwig Hausdorf claims that Voyager 2 - an unmanned
  interplanetary probe blasted into space 33 years ago - has
  started transmitting strange, unintelligible signals, Bild
  newspaper revealed today
  NASA installed a 12-inch disk containing music and greetings in
  55 languages in case intelligent extraterrestrial life ever
  found it.
  But last month the probe began sending back distorted messages
  from its location near the edge of the solar system that baffled
  NASA scientists were unable to decode, Mr Hausdorf says.
  "It seems almost as if someone had reprogrammed or hijacked the
  probe, thus perhaps we do not yet know the whole truth," the
  author of UFOs - They Are Still Flying told Bild.
  The signal from Voyager 2 - which takes 13 hours to reach the
  Earth - broke off fully on April 22.
  NASA said engineers were working to solve a data transmission
  fault.
  The space agency has not commented specifically on Mr Hausdorf's
  comments, although it says the fault is likely to be a glitch in
  the probe's computer memory
  Voyager 2 and its twin, Voyager 1, were launched in 1977 to
  explore Jupiter, Saturn, Uranus and Neptune. Three decades on,
  they are the most distant human-made objects in outer space.
  Voyager 1 is currently more than 8.5 billion miles from Earth.
  It will soon travel beyond the heliosphere - a bubble the sun
  creates around the solar system - into interstellar space,
  scientists say.
  -----

    "Send more Chuck Berry!"
    <VBG> --ebk]

New Shag Harbour Museum Opens June 1, 2010




                                                         UFO Updates
                                   A mailing list for the study of UFO-related phenomena
                                               'Its All Here In Black & White'
    Location: UFOUpDatesList.Com > 2010 > May > May 17

                                      New Shag Harbour Museum Opens June 1, 2010
  From: Don Ledger <dledger.nul>
  Date: Sun, 16 May 2010 14:52:22 -0300
  Archived: Mon, 17 May 2010 06:16:34 -0400
  Subject: New Shag Harbour Museum Opens June 1, 2010

  May 16, 2010
  The Shag Harbour UFO Incident Society has been making good
  progress at the new museum site in Shag Harbour. Municipal
  government grants have come through so that the Society could
  hire some workers. Interviews were carried out and two students
  have been hired for the summer season.
  The Society will open the museum site on June 1st with a Grand
  Opening on June 5th during the Lobster Fest.
  The new museum site - boasting two floors and over 3,000 square
  feet of space - puts it on the map as a good location for
  archiving material pertinant to the UFO event that occurred on
  Oct. 4, 1967 in Shag Harbour.
  The Shag Harbour UFO Incident Society's annual festival will
  take place on August 6 and 7. See the website for details at:
  www.shagharbourufo.com

  Don Ledger
  webmaster

Re: Project Blue Book - UFOB/Virginia

  >Don Ledger

  Don, it is a trip down memory lane and a bit of teletype history
  too. I'm an old teletype/AUTODIN (Automatic Digital Network)
  store and forward message switching guy. For months now I have
  been scanning through the PBB image files and repoking (fat-
  fingering) selected teletype messages for a virtual teletype
  network and mailing list.
  The tape relay network (those channel numbers of three letters
  and three numbers) evolved into Plan 55 in the 1950s and into
  AUTODIN in the late 1960s. I am familiar with most of the
  abbreviations, caveats, and other idiosyncrasies. For example,
  CAVU means ceiling and visibility unlimited.
  Another message describes three General Mills balloon scientists
  who observe an object while monitoring a test balloon through a
  theodolite. Since many UFO reports are explained as errant
  weather balloons (RAWINs and others), I found this a unique
  report.

  Terry

  theme) as overthrowing existing paradigms ( normal science ) and
  establishing new ones. The alien abduction experience, accepted
  in popular culture but dismissed by scientists, motivates this
  symposium about normal versus revolutionary science in

  >Presenters/Authors: Stuart Appelle, Susan Clancy, Budd Hopkins,
  >Roger K. Pitman
  >Symposium Location: Fairfax A
  >Don Donderi writes:
  >"Thomas Kuhn defined revolutionary science (the conference
  >theme) as overthrowing existing paradigms ( normal science ) and
  >establishing new ones. The alien abduction experience, accepted
  >in popular culture but dismissed by scientists, motivates this
  >symposium about normal versus revolutionary science in
  >psychology. Four prominent researchers of the abduction
  >phenomenon discuss the issues." See: http://tinyurl.com/2u5vdpu
  The same old, same old presenters. Especially, Clancy and
  Hopkins.
  The revolutionary side of things, at this conference, is not
  represented by revolutionary thinking. The field needs to allow
  out researchers voices to be heard. Not the same ones we have
  been hearing for the last 10 to 20 years. No wonder the field is
  not moving forward.

  "Alien Abduction Experiences: Normal Science or Revolutionary

  "Alien abduction reports and psychological science
  Stuart Appelle
  Dean, School of Science and Mathematics, State University
  of New York at Brockport
  Claims of alien abduction are generally dismissed by
  scientists out of hand. However neither evidence,
  scientific principle, nor implausibility arguments support an
  a priori dismissal. Rather, this typical response says more
  about the sociology of theory development in scientific
  psychology than it does about proper assessment of the
  phenomenon itself."
  "The Cognitive Psychology of Alien Abduction: Evidence for
  False Memories
  Susan Clancy
  INCAE Business School, Managua, Nicaragua
  =E2=80=9CAlien abductees=E2=80=9D are studied using variants of the Deese/
  Roediger-McDermott paradigm. They exhibit more false recall
  and recognition than controls. They are normal on correct
  recall and recognition. =E2=80=9CAlien abductees=E2=80=9D and people=
   reporting
  recovered trauma memories may be prone to creating false
  memories both inside and outside the lab."
  <snip>
  "Psychophysiological Non-Evidence for Alien Abduction
  Roger K. Pitman
  Harvard Medical School
  Like PTSD patients, when so-called alien abductees engage in
  mental imagery of their abduction events, they show elevated
  physiological responses. Does this mean these events actually

  occurred? Hardly. Emotional arousal for any reason can be
  reflected in physiological responses. Lie detectors show
  increased physiological responses when people are lying."

  To Mr. Appelle: Thank you for considering the rejection of "a

  trivializations themselves.
  To Mr. Pitman: Please consider that polygraphs seem to only
  measure physiological stresses on only those which it can
  measure physiological stresses. If this sounds circular, it is,
  since polygraphy is not science. There is no empirical evidence
  for polygraph accuracy because its accuracy does not generalize.

PRG Update - 5/19/10 - X-Conference 2010




                                                         UFO Updates
                                   A mailing list for the study of UFO-related phenomena

    Location: UFOUpDatesList.Com > 2010 > May > May 20

                                           PRG Update - 5/19/10 - X-Conference 2010
  From: Stephen Bassett <PRG.nul>
  Date: Wed, 19 May 2010 20:29:04 -0700
  Archived: Thu, 20 May 2010 06:18:19 -0400
  Subject: PRG Update - 5/19/10 - X-Conference 2010

  PRG
  Paradigm Research Group
  PRG Update - May 19, 2010 - X-Conference 2010
  X-Conference 2010 - Status
  www.x-conference.com
  The sixth X-Conference took place May 7-9 and was well received
  by those in attendance. It was the first conference (not a press
  conference) ever held on the UFO/ET issues at the National Press
  Club in Washington, DC. The NPC, a private club, has a long
  track record of being open minded on all issues of the day
  including those which are controversial. The Club was an
  excellent host and the facilities and food service were
  wonderful.
  The press conference on Monday morning went as planned. As it
  happens the White House scheduled its press conference for the
  new Supreme Court nominee at the same time. No matter. The PC
  was, of course, filmed and that video will get plenty of
  exposure. Right now you may see it courtesy of Frederik Uldall
  at:
  www.vimeo.com/11760935 and www.vimeo.com/1177787
  All presentations were strong, but those by Richard Dolan, Paul
  Stonehill, A. J. Gevaerd and Linda Moulton Howe/John Burroughs
  were of particular exopolitical significance, and PRG was
  pleased to get this information inside the Beltway.
  At the PRG Awards Banquet on Saturday evening the following
  individuals were honored: Courage in Journalism - Linda Moulton
  Howe; Political Courage - John Fife Symington, III; Intellectual
  Courage - Richard Dolan; Disclosure Award - Gary Heseltine (UK);
  Lifetime Achievement/Hall of Fame (posthumous) - Richard Hall;
  Lifetime Achievement/Hall of Fame (living) - A. J. Gevaerd
  (Brazil). George Knapp was presented the award for Courage in
  Journalism he received last year. A special PRG award was given
  to Dennis Briefer. More information on this will go up shortly
  at: www.paradigmresearchgroup.org/PRG_Awards.htm
  The banquet was privileged to be joined via Skype video by Dr.
  Edgar Mitchell who provided an update on what has happened since
  he called for the Obama administration to end the truth embargo
  on April 20, 2009.
  On Friday evening New Paradigm Films showed a pre-release
  version of its new documentary, The Day Before Disclosure.
  www.newparadigm.no
  Trailer: www.youtube.com/newparadigmfilms

  PRG/X-Conference Financial Status
  The economic turmoil and decline has taken its toll on everyone
  (except those primarily responsible for the turmoil and decline,
  as usual). PRG is no exception. It is not a business and the


http://www.ufoupdateslist.com/2010/may/m20-005.shtml[10/12/2011 21:06:19]
PRG Update - 5/19/10 - X-Conference 2010

  advocacy work has to go forward regardless of the ups, downs and
  insanity of U. S. financial institutions and policies.
  This year PRG comes out of the X-Conference $36,000 in debt and
  will immediately begin efforts to raise the funds to cover all
  outstanding balances. About $1200 is carried over from 2009.
  This is a gross amount and not net of any sizable money to PRG.
  PRG and its volunteer staff have received but negligible amounts
  for the thousands of hours of work that have gone into the six
  X-Conferences (less than $5000 total over six years).
  In due course every outstanding balance will be paid relating to
  the X-Conference's total expenditures of over $350,000 since
  2004. On an up note, PRG believes the prospects for a major
  funder to come forward who understands the critical need to end
  the truth embargo are much improved. Every day more people come
  to realize the monumental discrepancy between the government's
  position on the UFO/ET issue and the true reality of the
  situation. PRG's feels someone will move soon to provide the
  kind of funding that will take this advocacy movement to a much
  higher level of effectiveness and impact.

  X-Conference 2010 Webcast/Archive - Status
  http://tinyurl.com/26yprkn
  There were serious problems with the first efforts by PRG to
  webcast the X-Conference. The worst issue involved the delivery
  of emails to registrants with instructions, username and
  password. Online attendees were also impacted by the sound
  issues. PRG apologizes to any of the 350 online attendees who
  were affected by these issues and promises to make sure they are
  not repeated in future events.
  In response to the problems we are remastering the sound where
  possible and uploading improved files to the archive.
  Furthermore, the archive access has been extended through June
  30. Those wishing to watch X-Conference 2010 may continue to
  register at the above email address.
  It was very gratifying to PRG that the vast majority of our
  online guests were very understanding of these technical
  problems. Their patience and support was and is much
  appreciated.

  X-Conference DVD/Audio Tape Status
  www.sedonamediacompany.com
  www.cstapes.com (Creative Seminars)
  This year there were serious problems with the sound systems
  (not the fault of the NPC). PRG has been working to remaster the
  presentations where possible and is pleased to announce that
  most of the sound issues are being taken care of. DVDs can now
  be ordered from Sedona Media Company (including 2009). Audio
  tapes can be ordered from Creative Seminars (including 2004-
  2009). X-Conference 2004-2008 DVDs can be ordered from
  Paranormal Matrix at: www.paranormalmatrix.com

                                                        Secrecy News -- 05/20/10
  From: Steven Aftergood <saftergood.nul>
  Date: Thu, 20 May 2010 10:16:33 -0400
  Archived: Thu, 20 May 2010 13:49:02 -0400
  Subject: Secrecy News -- 05/20/10

  SECRECY NEWS
  from the FAS Project on Government Secrecy
  Volume 2010, Issue No. 41
  May 20, 2010
  Secrecy News Blog: http://www.fas.org/blog/secrecy/

  ** DOES CANDOR REQUIRE SECRECY? A CRITICAL REVIEW
  ** NECESSARY SECRETS: PANEL DISCUSSION
  ** JASON: BASIC RESEARCH AT THE PENTAGON IS "BROKEN"

  DOES CANDOR REQUIRE SECRECY? A CRITICAL REVIEW
  When the Supreme Court ordered the Nixon White House to comply
  with a subpoena for the Watergate tapes in the 1974 case of
  United States v. Nixon, it also endorsed the general proposition
  that secrecy is essential to presidential deliberations since it
  permits greater candor and therefore promotes a superior policy
  outcome. "A President and those who assist him must be free to
  explore alternatives in the process of shaping policies and
  making decisions and to do so in a way many would be unwilling
  to express except privately," the Court said.
  The premise that candor is incompatible with disclosure has
  become a cornerstone of the edifice of government secrecy, and
  an axiom of freedom of information policy, which provides an
  exemption for deliberative records. Yet it is mistaken,
  according to an iconoclastic new law review paper, and should be
  corrected.
  It seems intuitively obvious that private discussions lend
  themselves to greater candor than do public ones. In private,
  anyone might be more willing to reveal ignorance or uncertainty,
  to express personal emotion, or to consider risky or improbable
  alternatives.
  But this is "a highly contestable view of human nature," write
  Eric Lane, Frederick A.O. Schwarz, Jr., and Emily Berman, and it
  has little empirical basis. What's worse, they say, is that the
  equation of candor and confidentiality fails to take into
  account the corrosive effects of secrecy.
  In practice, according to the authors, secrecy may actually
  discourage candor. "When policy deliberations are deemed likely
  to remain secret, dissenters from the majority view might be
  more reluctant to give voice to their concerns.... And decision
  makers themselves might feel freer to silence dissenters when
  they do not expect their decision-making processes to be subject
  to scrutiny." This was often reportedly the case during the
  George W. Bush Administration when, according to multiple
  accounts cited in the paper, presidential advisors declined to
  question or to challenge confidential policy judgments.
  Furthermore, secrecy is not necessary for candor. Congress
  rarely invokes secrecy in its deliberations, though it is
  constitutionally authorized to do so. As is demonstrated in many
  political contexts, it is perfectly possible for policy


http://www.ufoupdateslist.com/2010/may/m20-009.shtml[10/12/2011 21:06:21]
Secrecy News -- 05/20/10

  discussions to be both open and candid, with no detrimental
  effect. (Under San Francisco's unusually bold 1999 Sunshine
  Ordinance, observed Joseph Lorenzo Hall of UC Berkeley,
  deliberative material is entirely subject to disclosure.)
  Finally, the authors write, candor itself does not necessarily
  promote good decision making. "While candor may have allowed the
  president to explore the possibility of engaging the CIA to
  interfere with an FBI investigation, surely such candor should
  not be encouraged by the promise of secrecy.... In many of the
  contexts in which candor is used as a justification for secrecy,
  the candor that is being shielded is candor that disserves the
  public interest."
  The authors emphasize that they do not categorically oppose
  confidential deliberations nor do they advocate that every
  official meeting be broadcast on live television. Rather, they
  argue that the presumption of deliberative secrecy adopted by
  the "Nixon" Court is unjustified by principle or practice, and
  that it should be replaced by a general presumption of openness,
  especially with respect to congressional requests for access to
  executive branch records.
  "The presumption established by the 'Nixon' Court endorsed and
  furthered a particular perception of the nature of government
  decision making -- that it is a process whose details should
  remain hidden behind a veil of secrecy.... It gives presidents
  and their advisors reason to believe that secrecy is standard
  operating procedure."
  "Dismantling the 'Nixon' canon -- as this Article advocates --
  would instead foster a culture where the expectations were
  reversed, where ideas about what is appropriate for public
  discussion are expanded, and where secrecy must be justified by
  a risk of significant harm -- not harm to the political
  prospects of the incumbent officials, but to the interests of
  the nation as a whole."
  See "Too Big a Canon in the President's Arsenal: Another Look at
  United States v. Nixon" by Eric Lane, Frederick A.O. Schwarz,
  Jr., and Emily Berman, George Mason Law Review, volume 17, no.
  3, Spring 2010:
  http://www.georgemasonlawreview.org/doc/17-3_Berman.pdf

  NECESSARY SECRETS: PANEL DISCUSSION
  The Hudson Institute will host a discussion of the new book
  "Necessary Secrets: National Security, the Media, and the Rule
  of Law" by Gabriel Schoenfeld on Tuesday, May 25. The book is a
  provocative account of the history and significance of "leaks"
  of classified information to the news media. The author laments
  the growing number and impact of such leaks, and generally
  favors more vigorous enforcement of laws against them. The May
  25 discussion will feature Mr. Schoenfeld, Benjamin Wittes of
  the Brookings Institution, and myself. There will be a keynote
  address by former CIA director Gen. Michael Hayden.

  http://www.hudson.org/index.cfm?fuseaction=3Dhudson_upcoming_events&id=3D773

  JASON: BASIC RESEARCH AT THE PENTAGON IS "BROKEN"
  Basic scientific research sponsored by the Department of Defense
  has suffered a precipitous decline in recent years, according to
  a newly disclosed 2009 report from the JASON defense advisory
  panel.
  "Basic research" refers to the investigation of fundamental
  phenomena, and contrasts with "applied research" that aims to
  meet a specific mission requirement or to solve a specified
  problem.
  "Over the past decade, there has been an exodus of scientific
  and technical expertise from the U.S. government and, in
  particular, from the DoD [basic] research enterprise," the
  JASONs said.
  "Gone are many of the technically literate program officers who


http://www.ufoupdateslist.com/2010/may/m20-009.shtml[10/12/2011 21:06:21]
Secrecy News -- 05/20/10

  plied the streets of the scientific community to find those
  remarkable people who could help shape the future. Gone too are
  many of the scientists and engineers in the academic community
  [who were supported by DoD basic research contracts] and who
  contributed to revolutionary advances that changed the landscape
  of modern war fighting. And most importantly, lost is the
  opportunity to develop the next generation of scientific talent
  who would otherwise have been trained and capable of carrying
  the research enterprise forward."
  =E2=80=9CDespite the importance of DoD Basic Research, we believe that
  important aspects of the DoD basic research programs are
  =E2=80=98broken=E2=80=99 to an extent that neither throwing more money at
  these problems nor simple changes in procedures and definitions
  will fix them,=E2=80=9D the report said.
  The JASONs nevertheless offer a series of recommendations
  concerning program organization and personnel recruitment to
  strengthen basic research. Among other things, they say that DoD
  should reject the "peer review" model for evaluating funding
  decisions, since that tends to reinforce the status quo, and
  should instead provide funding to exceptional individuals. They
  favorably cite Nobel laureate Luis Alvarez saying: "In my
  considered opinion the peer review system, in which proposals
  rather than proposers are reviewed, is the greatest disaster to
  be visited upon the scientific community this century...."
  The JASON report was originally marked "for official use only."
  When the Federation of American Scientists requested it last
  year under the Freedom of Information Act, most of the document
  was withheld as "deliberative." But upon appeal, DoD agreed this
  month to release the entire report. To accompany the release,
  Alan R. Shaffer, Director of Defense Research and Engineering,
  issued a cover memorandum stating that the JASON report was "one
  perspective" among several and that it was not based on a
  comprehensive data set.
  See "S & T for National Security," JASON Summer Study, completed
  May 2009, released May 2010:
  http://www.fas.org/irp/agency/dod/jason/sandt-full.pdf

  Texas State MUFON Director Ken Cherry, prominent in the media
  during the Stephenville UFO sightings, is not a modest man.
  Anyone who has met him knows this. On May 2nd he called a
  meeting of the DFW MUFON chapter titled FOIA Ain't Free:
  Stephenville, Tx - Part Ii: Video And Lecture, $4 for members,
  $5 for non-members.
  Only an hour of the 2:40 meeting concerned radar analyses of two
  flaps, in January and October 2008. The bulk of the meeting was
  taken by Cherry, on a soap box, attacking ufology and mainstream
  media, outing and belittling witnesses, those in the military,
  attacking MUFON itself, and anyone who does not see the world
  exactly as Mr. Cherry sees it.
  As proof of his claims that the widely heralded Schulze/Powell
  radar report (the one commissioned by the National MUFON office,
  not his own) is hopelessly flawed, he and Asst. Dir. Steve
  Hudgeons pass out copies of a blog by UFO naysayer Tim Printy,
  who believes the entire wave of sightings were military jets and
  hysteria.
  Cherry let it be known that he does not think ordinary Joes can
  give credible sighting accounts.
  Cherry’s statements and attempted defense of them by Hudgeons
  and Steve Reichmuth have MUFON membership in an uproar.
  http://mufonmembers.proboards.com
  A 2-part YouTube video has been released, documenting the many
  violations of MUFON rules, ethics, and Mr. Cherry’s overall lack
  of respect for almost anyone aside from himself. His own words,
  recorded at the meeting, are worth hearing. The complete audio
  and transcript of the meeting are being released in sequence on
  YouTube as well.
  The charges, Part 1:
  http://www.youtube.com/watch?v=szijupP1ZS8
  Part 2:
  http://www.youtube.com/watch?v=KmlIWp29G5I
  Also included there will be Bill Puckett’s newly performed (but
  not freely released) radar analysis of the October 2008
  Stephenville sightings.

                                          My Conclusions Regarding ET Phenomena
  From: Lyle Michel <ljm1915.nul>
  Date: Sat, 22 May 2010 10:36:04 -0700
  Archived: Sat, 22 May 2010 13:53:58 -0400
  Subject: My Conclusions Regarding ET Phenomena

  Near the end of the following article is a paragraph that
  begins, "Liberal Senator Colin Kenny was approached by Hellyer
  in late 2005..."
  According to this article Kenny chaired the Senate defense
  committee in Canada. If we take Kenny at his word he seemingly
  knows absolutely nothing about the extraterrestrials that are
  coming to earth and is absolutely convinced that they are not
  here.
  I don't know how some of you feel, but what would you think if
  President Obama were to go on national television and nominate
  someone as Secretary of Defense here in America and his
  credentials were that he knows absolutely nothing about the
  defense industry, was never in the American military and had no
  experience at the position that he was being hired to head. I
  think most people would wonder what the heck the president was
  doing nominating such an unqualified person for such and
  important job.
  The point I am trying to make is that the article suggests that
  Senator Colin Kenny actually knows something about the
  extraterrestrial phenomena, when as far as I know their is not a
  shred of evidence to support his position.
  Among the evidence to support the fact that extraterrestrials
  are coming to earth are hundreds if not thousands of photographs
  of their vehicles. There are likely millions of witnesses
  around the world of people, such as myself that have seen their
  craft including astronauts like Gordon Cooper and others (I
  would consider an astronauts testimony to be valuable and
  truthful, wouldn't you.) Also we have experiencers by the
  score; some of whom I have spent many hours with discussing
  their experiences with the extraterrestrials. We have actual
  physical evidence such as the implants that Dr Lear has taken
  out of these experiencers to support their statements.
  We have conferences all over the United States such as the
  conference being hosted by Ike Bishop over this coming Memorial
  weekend in Alamo Nevada. At these conferences professionals, who
  have spent decades of their life studying the extraterrestrials,
  give testimony about their personal experiences such as seeing
  extraterrestrials with their own eyes when they showed up in
  their living room. There is also court room like evidence
  testimony of the experiencers who have been with the
  extraterrestrials on multiple occasions. In other words the
  professional, such as Barbara Lamb who will speak at the Alamo
  conference, is stating that in their judgment the person is
  telling the truth regarding their experience and contact with
  extraterrestrials.
  In a criminal case psychiatrist often testify that the person is
  of sound mind and telling the truth. If expert testimony is good
  enough for our court rooms it should be good enough to establish
  the credibility of extraterrestrial contactees.
  One of my most amazing discoveries in the 18 years of studying
  the phenomena is that seemingly people who have absolutely no
  knowledge of the extraterrestrials, have no interest in knowing


http://www.ufoupdateslist.com/2010/may/m22-006.shtml[10/12/2011 21:06:25]
My Conclusions Regarding ET Phenomena

  anything about these beings, are thought to be the expert, and
  are certain that their is no truth to the fact that beings from
  other worlds are coming to earth. They make statements like
  this although there is not a shred of evidence to support their
  position.
  I would like to ask the group if they are aware of any other
  field of study or profession where the person who knows
  absolutely nothing about the subject is thought to be the
  expert. If you do please write to me and let me know.
  How many of you choose your medical doctor because he/she had
  never studied in the medical field, had no experience diagnosing
  illnesses or treating patients, and was not licensed to practice
  medicine anywhere in the United States.
  How often have we seen the press select someone as an expert on
  the extraterrestrial phenomena who publicly knows nothing about
  the subject matter.
  Comments welcome...

  http://tinyurl.com/2wrxl4z
  The Canadian Press
  Date: Sunday May. 2, 2010 3:32 PM ET
  MONTREAL =97 Stephen Hawking's warnings of an alien invasion have
  prompted a vigorous defence of extraterrestrials by their most
  prominent Canadian fan.
  Former federal defence minister Paul Hellyer, 86, believes not
  only that aliens have visited Earth but also that they have
  contributed greatly to human technological advances.
  So he can't quite understand why the world renowned
  astrophysicist views them with such trepidation; Hawking
  recently warned that malevolent aliens could lead to the
  destruction of humanity.
  The longtime cabinet minister accuses Hawking of spreading
  misinformation about extraterrestrials.
  "I think he's indulging in some pretty scary talk there that I
  would have hoped would not come from someone with such an
  established stature," Hellyer said in an interview.
  "I think it's really sad that a scientist of his repute would
  contribute to what I would consider more misinformation about a
  vast and very important subject."
  Hawking speculates in a new documentary that most
  extraterrestrial life will be similar to microbes, or small
  animals -- but he adds that advanced life forms may be "nomads,
  looking to conquer and colonize" new planets.
  Hellyer served in the Liberal cabinets of Louis St. Laurent,
  Lester Pearson and Pierre Trudeau, later crossed the floor to
  the Conservative party, and eventually returned to the Liberals.
  Hellyer, who ran for the Liberal party leadership in 1968, has
  been speaking out publicly about "the star visitors" for years.
  His most recent speech was in Washington in April 2009.
  He dismisses a suggestion that some people might find his views
  a little hard to believe.
  "The reality is that they've (aliens) been visiting Earth for
  decades and probably millennia and have contributed considerably
  to our knowledge," Hellyer said.
  He says earthlings owe a lot to their alien friends. Even that
  computer screen on your desk, he says, can trace its origins to
  spaceships.
  "Microchips, for example, fiber-optics, they are just two of the
  many things that allegedly -- and probably for real -- came from
  crashed vehicles," Hellyer said.


http://www.ufoupdateslist.com/2010/may/m22-006.shtml[10/12/2011 21:06:25]
My Conclusions Regarding ET Phenomena


  Hellyer admits that when he was defence minister, he never got
  any briefings on UFOs from the military. He says he got reports
  of sightings, and that some of them could not be explained.
  His interest in aliens goes back at least as far as 1967,
  Canada's centennial year.
  That's the year Hellyer went to St. Paul, Alta., to officially
  inaugurate the world's first flying saucer landing pad which was
  built by the locals to celebrate the centennial.
  The town continues to get about 5,000 visitors a year, with more
  than half making a pilgrimage to the landing pad.
  Liberal Senator Colin Kenny was approached by Hellyer in late
  2005 and asked to hold public hearings on Canadian exopolitics.
  That's the politics of how to deal with extraterrestrial
  contact.
  Kenny, who chaired the Senate defence committee, remembers the
  meeting well.
  "I liked Paul, I worked for him in the leadership race in 1968,
  and when he called me up to see me I was pleased to see him and
  we reminisced for a while," Kenny recalls.
  "Then, wow! Out of the blue, he starts talking about
  extraterrestrials and how they're everywhere and they have these
  immense powers."
  Kenny says his opinion about aliens hasn't changed since that
  meeting five years ago: "I think it's for the birds."
  "I'm certainly not going to waste any of my time on the
  extraterrestrial business," Kenny said.
  A past president of the Royal Astronomical Society of Canada
  agrees there might be UFOs out there, but he's a little more
  hesitant about aliens. He takes Hawking's warning with a grain
  of salt.
  "I think it's an opinion more than anything," Randy Attwood
  said.
  "I think he may be throwing the ball out into the field, just to
  try to get people to actually start talking about this."
  Attwood says data gathered during the exploration of Mars by
  unmanned spacecraft give some credence to Hawking, who is an
  honorary member of the astronomical society.
  "All things are pointing now to life on Mars in the past,
  possibly when its orbit was different," he said. "It had an
  atmosphere, it as warmer, there was running water."
  "So it's quite possible that life was in fact there -- not
  Martian wilder beasts or anything like that -- but multiple-cell
  organisms."
  Attwood says given that one of Earth's closest neighbours
  potentially had life, it's quite possible that life, in fact,
  exists elsewhere in the universe.
  "Whether it's single-celled or multiple-cell, or beings that are
  actually travelling through space, we don't know," he said
  -----

  The senate in Canada is a chamber of so-called sober second
  thought that is able to recommend changes to new legislation
  proposed by the house of commons. It does not pass laws like the
  US senate. This does not excuse Mr. Kenny's apparent ignorance,
  but puts his possible influence on public policy in perspective.
  I think most people in the political arena follow rather than
  lead public opinion to try to keep the respect of the majority
  of the population. A comment that ETs are real would be
  perceived as leading rather than following, hence, is risky.
  People like Mr. Kenny might expect such a comment to negatively
  affect their reputations and the reputations of the political
  institutions they represent. So no one should be surprised at
  Mr. Kenny's reported opinion that the reality of ETs is "for the
  birds". That view will likely not change until he perceives
  mainstream support for such a change.

  William

  >I think most people would wonder what the heck the president was
  >doing nominating such an unqualified person for such and
  >important job.
  In order for knowledge of aliens to be part of the job
  description, there would first need to be a formal
  acknowledgement that aliens are real and here. In other words,
  disclosure would need to take place first. Without disclosure
  first, and in the absence of first hand experience with aliens,
  it would be a far greater concern to have somebody claiming
  belief in alien visitation in a position of such responsibility.
  There would be no clear and logical reason for anyone to accept
  the person concerned has come to such belief from a sound state
  of mind.
  >Among the evidence to support the fact that extraterrestrials
  >are coming to earth are hundreds if not thousands of photographs
  >of their vehicles.
  Unfortunately the vast majority are taken with cardboard pinhole
  cameras by people unable to stand still, while the clear close
  up ones are all computer generated or models.
  >At these conferences professionals, who
  >have spent decades of their life studying the extraterrestrials
  Are there research facilities with aliens? How do you study
  something when the subject matter is absent? What people study
  is information, which ultimately cannot be proven. One does not
  become a car mechanic without ever seeing, let alone touching, a
  car.
  >In a criminal case psychiatrist often testify that the person is
  >of sound mind and telling the truth. If expert testimony is good
  >enough for our court rooms it should be good enough to establish
  >the credibility of extraterrestrial contactees.
  Testimony in conjunction with hands on evidence. Testimony
  without a proven actual event is meaningless.
  >How many of you choose your medical doctor because he/she had
  >never studied in the medical field, had no experience diagnosing
  >illnesses or treating patients, and was not licensed to practice
  >medicine anywhere in the United States.
  How many doctors are required to be expert at something the
  medical profession doesn't even know about?
  Don't get me wrong, I'm not saying aliens are not visiting. But
  you seem to believe that the world should operate as though
  disclosure has already taken place, and alien visitation is
  confirmed fact, accepted by all officialdom and the public. It
  isn't.

Rock 'n' Roll, Comics & UFOs
